scp -r '/home/ckw/Downloads/nvidia/sdkm_downloads/nvidia-l4t-gstreamer_39.2.0-20260601141651_arm64.deb' jetson@[192.168.55.1]:/opt/nvidia/deb_repos/

#****** beginning of  /home/ckw/.nvsdkm/replays/scripts/JetPack_7.2_Linux/NV_L4T_GSTREAMER_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh ******

#!/bin/bash
set -e
export LC_ALL=en_US.UTF-8 LANG=en_US.UTF-8 LANGUAGE=en_US.UTF-8

exec 3>&1
exec 1>&2
exec 2>&3
function safe_apt_install {
    export DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive
    echo "*begin $1, $2"
    tmp_log=/tmp/sdkm_apt_install_log.ckw.txt
    sudo rm -f $tmp_log
    set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
    MAX_RETRY=3
    TIMES=1
    while [ ${CMD_STATUS} -ne 0 ]; do
        cat $tmp_log > /dev/stderr
        if [ $TIMES -gt $MAX_RETRY ]; then
            echo "Unhandled error when running $1: `tr '\n' '; ' <$tmp_log`" 1>&2; exit 1
        fi
        if grep -q -e "is locked by another process" -e "E: Could not get lock /var/" -e "Could not open lock" $tmp_log; then
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting $2 seconds for apt to be available"; sleep ${2}
        elif grep -q -e "apt --fix-broken install" $tmp_log; then
            echo "safe apt install failed, need to run apt --fix-broken install"
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; sudo apt-get -f -y --allow-downgrades install 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting 5 seconds for apt"; sleep 5
        else
            echo "safe apt install failed: $1, exit code: $CMD_STATUS, retry number: $TIMES"
            echo "will sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ] seconds before next attempt"
            sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ]
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
        fi
    done
    if [ $CMD_STATUS -eq 0 ] && [ $TIMES -ne 1 ]; then
        echo "safe apt install succeed. retry number: $TIMES."
    fi
}
safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get -y update" 5
sleep 0.5
safe_apt_install "sudo -E apt-get -y --allow-downgrades  install /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/nvidia-l4t-gstreamer_39.2.0-20260601141651_arm64.deb" 5
sleep 5
sudo rm -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/nvidia-l4t-gstreamer_39.2.0-20260601141651_arm64.deb

#****** end of  /home/ckw/.nvsdkm/replays/scripts/JetPack_7.2_Linux/NV_L4T_GSTREAMER_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh ******

cd /tmp
ssh jetson@192.168.55.1 "dpkg-query -W -f='\${db:Status-abbrev} \${Version}' nvidia-l4t-gstreamer"
ssh   jetson@192.168.55.1 "df -B1 / | tail -1 | awk '{ printf \$4 }'"
export _xdk_type=

ssh   jetson@192.168.55.1 "df -B1 / | tail -1 | awk '{ printf \$4 }'"

#****** beginning of  /home/ckw/.nvsdkm/replays/scripts/JetPack_7.2_Linux/NV_L4T_DOCKER_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh ******

#!/bin/bash
set -e
export LC_ALL=en_US.UTF-8 LANG=en_US.UTF-8 LANGUAGE=en_US.UTF-8

exec 3>&1
exec 1>&2
exec 2>&3
function safe_apt_install {
    export DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive
    echo "*begin $1, $2"
    tmp_log=/tmp/sdkm_apt_install_log.ckw.txt
    sudo rm -f $tmp_log
    set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
    MAX_RETRY=3
    TIMES=1
    while [ ${CMD_STATUS} -ne 0 ]; do
        cat $tmp_log > /dev/stderr
        if [ $TIMES -gt $MAX_RETRY ]; then
            echo "Unhandled error when running $1: `tr '\n' '; ' <$tmp_log`" 1>&2; exit 1
        fi
        if grep -q -e "is locked by another process" -e "E: Could not get lock /var/" -e "Could not open lock" $tmp_log; then
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting $2 seconds for apt to be available"; sleep ${2}
        elif grep -q -e "apt --fix-broken install" $tmp_log; then
            echo "safe apt install failed, need to run apt --fix-broken install"
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; sudo apt-get -f -y --allow-downgrades install 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting 5 seconds for apt"; sleep 5
        else
            echo "safe apt install failed: $1, exit code: $CMD_STATUS, retry number: $TIMES"
            echo "will sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ] seconds before next attempt"
            sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ]
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
        fi
    done
    if [ $CMD_STATUS -eq 0 ] && [ $TIMES -ne 1 ]; then
        echo "safe apt install succeed. retry number: $TIMES."
    fi
}
safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get -y update" 5
sleep 0.5
safe_apt_install "sudo -E apt-get -y --allow-downgrades  install /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/libnvidia-container-tools_1.19.1-1_arm64.deb" 5
sleep 5
sudo rm -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/libnvidia-container-tools_1.19.1-1_arm64.deb

#****** end of  /home/ckw/.nvsdkm/replays/scripts/JetPack_7.2_Linux/NV_L4T_DOCKER_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh ******


scp /home/ckw/.nvsdkm/replays/scripts/JetPack_7.2_Linux/NV_L4T_DOCKER_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh jetson@[192.168.55.1]:~; ssh -t jetson@192.168.55.1 "set -o pipefail; ~/NV_L4T_DOCKER_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh | sed 's/^/SDKM_INSTALL_ERROR /' && rm -f ~/NV_L4T_DOCKER_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh"
cd /tmp
ssh jetson@192.168.55.1 "dpkg-query -W -f='\${db:Status-abbrev} \${Version}' libnvidia-container-tools"

#!/bin/bash
set -e
export LC_ALL=en_US.UTF-8 LANG=en_US.UTF-8 LANGUAGE=en_US.UTF-8

exec 3>&1
exec 1>&2
exec 2>&3
function safe_apt_install {
    export DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive
    echo "*begin $1, $2"
    tmp_log=/tmp/sdkm_apt_install_log.ckw.txt
    sudo rm -f $tmp_log
    set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
    MAX_RETRY=3
    TIMES=1
    while [ ${CMD_STATUS} -ne 0 ]; do
        cat $tmp_log > /dev/stderr
        if [ $TIMES -gt $MAX_RETRY ]; then
            echo "Unhandled error when running $1: `tr '\n' '; ' <$tmp_log`" 1>&2; exit 1
        fi
        if grep -q -e "is locked by another process" -e "E: Could not get lock /var/" -e "Could not open lock" $tmp_log; then
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting $2 seconds for apt to be available"; sleep ${2}
        elif grep -q -e "apt --fix-broken install" $tmp_log; then
            echo "safe apt install failed, need to run apt --fix-broken install"
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; sudo apt-get -f -y --allow-downgrades install 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting 5 seconds for apt"; sleep 5
        else
            echo "safe apt install failed: $1, exit code: $CMD_STATUS, retry number: $TIMES"
            echo "will sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ] seconds before next attempt"
            sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ]
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
        fi
    done
    if [ $CMD_STATUS -eq 0 ] && [ $TIMES -ne 1 ]; then
        echo "safe apt install succeed. retry number: $TIMES."
    fi
}
safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get -y update" 5
sleep 0.5
safe_apt_install "sudo -E apt-get -y --allow-downgrades  install /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/cuda-repo-ubuntu2404-13-2-local_13.2.1-595.58.03-1_arm64.deb" 5
sleep 5
# ensure package version from local repository has a higher priority
sudo mkdir -p /etc/apt/preferences.d/
sudo rm -f /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm
sudo touch /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm
echo 'Package: *' | sudo tee -a /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm
echo 'Pin: origin ""' | sudo tee -a /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm
echo 'Pin-Priority: 999' | sudo tee -a /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm

packageName=$(dpkg -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/cuda-repo-ubuntu2404-13-2-local_13.2.1-595.58.03-1_arm64.deb Package)
keyringFilePath=$(dpkg -L $packageName | grep 'keyring\.gpg$' | head -n 1 | tr -d "\n")
if [ -n "$keyringFilePath" ]
then
	if [ -f "$keyringFilePath" ]
	then
		sudo cp $keyringFilePath /usr/share/keyrings/
	else
		echo 'Warning: deb repo keyring file not found' >&2
	fi
else
	pubKeyFilePath=$(dpkg -L $packageName | grep '\.pub$' | head -n 1 | tr -d "\n")
	echo $pubKeyFilePath
	if [ -n "$pubKeyFilePath" ]
	then
		safe_apt_install "sudo apt-key add $pubKeyFilePath" 5
	else
		echo 'Warning: deb repo pub key file not found' >&2
	fi
fi
sleep 2
safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get -y update" 5

safe_apt_install "sudo -E apt-get -y  install cuda-cudart-dev-13-2 cuda-driver-dev-13-2 " 5
targetDebRepoPackageName=$(dpkg -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/cuda-repo-ubuntu2404-13-2-local_13.2.1-595.58.03-1_arm64.deb Package | tr -d '\n')
echo targetDebRepoPackageName $targetDebRepoPackageName
if [ -n "targetDebRepoPackageName" ]
then
    safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get purge -y $targetDebRepoPackageName" 5
else
    echo 'Warning: target deb repo package name not found' >&2
fi
sudo rm -f /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm

sudo rm -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/cuda-repo-ubuntu2404-13-2-local_13.2.1-595.58.03-1_arm64.deb

#****** end of  /home/ckw/.nvsdkm/replays/scripts/JetPack_7.2_Linux/NV_L4T_CUDA_RUNTIME_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh ******

cd /tmp
ssh jetson@192.168.55.1 "dpkg-query -W -f='\${db:Status-abbrev} \${Version}' cuda-cudart-dev-13-2"
cd /tmp
ssh jetson@192.168.55.1 "dpkg-query -W -f='\${db:Status-abbrev} \${Version}' cuda-driver-dev-13-2"
scp  /home/ckw/.nvsdkm/replays/scripts/JetPack_7.2_Linux/NV_L4T_CUDA_RUNTIME_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh jetson@[192.168.55.1]:~; ssh -t  jetson@192.168.55.1 "set -o pipefail; ~/NV_L4T_CUDA_RUNTIME_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh | sed 's/^/SDKM_INSTALL_ERROR /' && rm -f ~/NV_L4T_CUDA_RUNTIME_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh"

#!/bin/bash
set -e
export LC_ALL=en_US.UTF-8 LANG=en_US.UTF-8 LANGUAGE=en_US.UTF-8

exec 3>&1
exec 1>&2
exec 2>&3
function safe_apt_install {
    export DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive
    echo "*begin $1, $2"
    tmp_log=/tmp/sdkm_apt_install_log.ckw.txt
    sudo rm -f $tmp_log
    set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
    MAX_RETRY=3
    TIMES=1
    while [ ${CMD_STATUS} -ne 0 ]; do
        cat $tmp_log > /dev/stderr
        if [ $TIMES -gt $MAX_RETRY ]; then
            echo "Unhandled error when running $1: `tr '\n' '; ' <$tmp_log`" 1>&2; exit 1
        fi
        if grep -q -e "is locked by another process" -e "E: Could not get lock /var/" -e "Could not open lock" $tmp_log; then
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting $2 seconds for apt to be available"; sleep ${2}
        elif grep -q -e "apt --fix-broken install" $tmp_log; then
            echo "safe apt install failed, need to run apt --fix-broken install"
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; sudo apt-get -f -y --allow-downgrades install 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting 5 seconds for apt"; sleep 5
        else
            echo "safe apt install failed: $1, exit code: $CMD_STATUS, retry number: $TIMES"
            echo "will sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ] seconds before next attempt"
            sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ]
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
        fi
    done
    if [ $CMD_STATUS -eq 0 ] && [ $TIMES -ne 1 ]; then
        echo "safe apt install succeed. retry number: $TIMES."
    fi
}
safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get -y update" 5
sleep 0.5
safe_apt_install "sudo -E apt-get -y --allow-downgrades  install /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/nvidia-l4t-cudadebuggingsupport_13.2-37290934.0_arm64.deb" 5
sleep 5
sudo rm -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/nvidia-l4t-cudadebuggingsupport_13.2-37290934.0_arm64.deb

#****** end of  /home/ckw/.nvsdkm/replays/scripts/JetPack_7.2_Linux/NV_L4T_CUDA_RUNTIME_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh ******

cd /tmp
ssh jetson@192.168.55.1 "dpkg-query -W -f='\${db:Status-abbrev} \${Version}' nvidia-l4t-cudadebuggingsupport"

#!/bin/bash
set -e
export LC_ALL=en_US.UTF-8 LANG=en_US.UTF-8 LANGUAGE=en_US.UTF-8

exec 3>&1
exec 1>&2
exec 2>&3
function safe_apt_install {
    export DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive
    echo "*begin $1, $2"
    tmp_log=/tmp/sdkm_apt_install_log.ckw.txt
    sudo rm -f $tmp_log
    set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
    MAX_RETRY=3
    TIMES=1
    while [ ${CMD_STATUS} -ne 0 ]; do
        cat $tmp_log > /dev/stderr
        if [ $TIMES -gt $MAX_RETRY ]; then
            echo "Unhandled error when running $1: `tr '\n' '; ' <$tmp_log`" 1>&2; exit 1
        fi
        if grep -q -e "is locked by another process" -e "E: Could not get lock /var/" -e "Could not open lock" $tmp_log; then
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting $2 seconds for apt to be available"; sleep ${2}
        elif grep -q -e "apt --fix-broken install" $tmp_log; then
            echo "safe apt install failed, need to run apt --fix-broken install"
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; sudo apt-get -f -y --allow-downgrades install 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting 5 seconds for apt"; sleep 5
        else
            echo "safe apt install failed: $1, exit code: $CMD_STATUS, retry number: $TIMES"
            echo "will sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ] seconds before next attempt"
            sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ]
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
        fi
    done
    if [ $CMD_STATUS -eq 0 ] && [ $TIMES -ne 1 ]; then
        echo "safe apt install succeed. retry number: $TIMES."
    fi
}
safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get -y update" 5
sleep 0.5
safe_apt_install "sudo -E apt-get -y --allow-downgrades  install /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/OpenCV-4.8.0-3-g6ef37b4-aarch64-python.deb" 5
sleep 5
sudo rm -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/OpenCV-4.8.0-3-g6ef37b4-aarch64-python.deb

#****** end of  /home/ckw/.nvsdkm/replays/scripts/JetPack_7.2_Linux/NV_L4T_OPENCV_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh ******

cd /tmp
ssh jetson@192.168.55.1 "dpkg-query -W -f='\${db:Status-abbrev} \${Version}' libopencv-python"

        else
            echo "safe apt install failed: $1, exit code: $CMD_STATUS, retry number: $TIMES"
            echo "will sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ] seconds before next attempt"
            sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ]
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
        fi
    done
    if [ $CMD_STATUS -eq 0 ] && [ $TIMES -ne 1 ]; then
        echo "safe apt install succeed. retry number: $TIMES."
    fi
}
safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get -y update" 5
sleep 0.5
safe_apt_install "sudo -E apt-get -y --allow-downgrades  install /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/pva-allow-2_2.0.5_all.deb" 5
sleep 5
sudo rm -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/pva-allow-2_2.0.5_all.deb

#!/bin/bash
set -e
export LC_ALL=en_US.UTF-8 LANG=en_US.UTF-8 LANGUAGE=en_US.UTF-8

exec 3>&1
exec 1>&2
exec 2>&3
function safe_apt_install {
    export DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive
    echo "*begin $1, $2"
    tmp_log=/tmp/sdkm_apt_install_log.ckw.txt
    sudo rm -f $tmp_log
    set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
    MAX_RETRY=3
    TIMES=1
    while [ ${CMD_STATUS} -ne 0 ]; do
        cat $tmp_log > /dev/stderr
        if [ $TIMES -gt $MAX_RETRY ]; then
            echo "Unhandled error when running $1: `tr '\n' '; ' <$tmp_log`" 1>&2; exit 1
        fi
        if grep -q -e "is locked by another process" -e "E: Could not get lock /var/" -e "Could not open lock" $tmp_log; then
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting $2 seconds for apt to be available"; sleep ${2}
        elif grep -q -e "apt --fix-broken install" $tmp_log; then
            echo "safe apt install failed, need to run apt --fix-broken install"
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; sudo apt-get -f -y --allow-downgrades install 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting 5 seconds for apt"; sleep 5
        else
            echo "safe apt install failed: $1, exit code: $CMD_STATUS, retry number: $TIMES"
            echo "will sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ] seconds before next attempt"
            sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ]
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
        fi
    done
    if [ $CMD_STATUS -eq 0 ] && [ $TIMES -ne 1 ]; then
        echo "safe apt install succeed. retry number: $TIMES."
    fi
}
safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get -y update" 5
sleep 0.5
safe_apt_install "sudo -E apt-get -y --allow-downgrades  install /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/vpi-lib-4.1.3-aarch64-l4t.deb" 5
sleep 5
sudo rm -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/vpi-lib-4.1.3-aarch64-l4t.deb

#****** end of  /home/ckw/.nvsdkm/replays/scripts/JetPack_7.2_Linux/NV_L4T_VPI_RUNTIME_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh ******

    export DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive
    echo "*begin $1, $2"
    tmp_log=/tmp/sdkm_apt_install_log.ckw.txt
    sudo rm -f $tmp_log
    set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e

    TIMES=1
    while [ ${CMD_STATUS} -ne 0 ]; do
        cat $tmp_log > /dev/stderr
        if [ $TIMES -gt $MAX_RETRY ]; then
            echo "Unhandled error when running $1: `tr '\n' '; ' <$tmp_log`" 1>&2; exit 1
        fi
        if grep -q -e "is locked by another process" -e "E: Could not get lock /var/" -e "Could not open lock" $tmp_log; then
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting $2 seconds for apt to be available"; sleep ${2}

            echo "safe apt install failed, need to run apt --fix-broken install"
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; sudo apt-get -f -y --allow-downgrades install 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting 5 seconds for apt"; sleep 5
        else
            echo "safe apt install failed: $1, exit code: $CMD_STATUS, retry number: $TIMES"
            echo "will sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ] seconds before next attempt"
            sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ]
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
        fi
    done
    if [ $CMD_STATUS -eq 0 ] && [ $TIMES -ne 1 ]; then
        echo "safe apt install succeed. retry number: $TIMES."
    fi
}

sleep 0.5

#!/bin/bash
set -e
export LC_ALL=en_US.UTF-8 LANG=en_US.UTF-8 LANGUAGE=en_US.UTF-8

exec 3>&1
exec 1>&2
exec 2>&3
function safe_apt_install {
    export DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive
    echo "*begin $1, $2"
    tmp_log=/tmp/sdkm_apt_install_log.ckw.txt
    sudo rm -f $tmp_log
    set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
    MAX_RETRY=3
    TIMES=1
    while [ ${CMD_STATUS} -ne 0 ]; do
        cat $tmp_log > /dev/stderr
        if [ $TIMES -gt $MAX_RETRY ]; then
            echo "Unhandled error when running $1: `tr '\n' '; ' <$tmp_log`" 1>&2; exit 1
        fi
        if grep -q -e "is locked by another process" -e "E: Could not get lock /var/" -e "Could not open lock" $tmp_log; then
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting $2 seconds for apt to be available"; sleep ${2}
        elif grep -q -e "apt --fix-broken install" $tmp_log; then
            echo "safe apt install failed, need to run apt --fix-broken install"
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; sudo apt-get -f -y --allow-downgrades install 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting 5 seconds for apt"; sleep 5
        else
            echo "safe apt install failed: $1, exit code: $CMD_STATUS, retry number: $TIMES"
            echo "will sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ] seconds before next attempt"
            sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ]
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
        fi
    done
    if [ $CMD_STATUS -eq 0 ] && [ $TIMES -ne 1 ]; then
        echo "safe apt install succeed. retry number: $TIMES."
    fi
}
safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get -y update" 5
sleep 0.5
safe_apt_install "sudo -E apt-get -y --allow-downgrades  install /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/cuda-repo-ubuntu2404-13-2-local_13.2.1-595.58.03-1_arm64.deb" 5
sleep 5
# ensure package version from local repository has a higher priority
sudo mkdir -p /etc/apt/preferences.d/
sudo rm -f /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm
sudo touch /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm
echo 'Package: *' | sudo tee -a /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm
echo 'Pin: origin ""' | sudo tee -a /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm
echo 'Pin-Priority: 999' | sudo tee -a /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm

packageName=$(dpkg -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/cuda-repo-ubuntu2404-13-2-local_13.2.1-595.58.03-1_arm64.deb Package)
keyringFilePath=$(dpkg -L $packageName | grep 'keyring\.gpg$' | head -n 1 | tr -d "\n")
if [ -n "$keyringFilePath" ]
then
	if [ -f "$keyringFilePath" ]
	then
		sudo cp $keyringFilePath /usr/share/keyrings/
	else
		echo 'Warning: deb repo keyring file not found' >&2
	fi
else
	pubKeyFilePath=$(dpkg -L $packageName | grep '\.pub$' | head -n 1 | tr -d "\n")
	echo $pubKeyFilePath
	if [ -n "$pubKeyFilePath" ]
	then
		safe_apt_install "sudo apt-key add $pubKeyFilePath" 5
	else
		echo 'Warning: deb repo pub key file not found' >&2
	fi
fi
sleep 2
safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get -y update" 5

safe_apt_install "sudo -E apt-get -y  install cuda-toolkit-13-2 " 5
targetDebRepoPackageName=$(dpkg -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/cuda-repo-ubuntu2404-13-2-local_13.2.1-595.58.03-1_arm64.deb Package | tr -d '\n')
echo targetDebRepoPackageName $targetDebRepoPackageName
if [ -n "targetDebRepoPackageName" ]
then
    safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get purge -y $targetDebRepoPackageName" 5
else
    echo 'Warning: target deb repo package name not found' >&2
fi
sudo rm -f /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm

sudo rm -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/cuda-repo-ubuntu2404-13-2-local_13.2.1-595.58.03-1_arm64.deb

#!/bin/bash
set -e
export LC_ALL=en_US.UTF-8 LANG=en_US.UTF-8 LANGUAGE=en_US.UTF-8

exec 3>&1
exec 1>&2
exec 2>&3
function safe_apt_install {
    export DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive
    echo "*begin $1, $2"
    tmp_log=/tmp/sdkm_apt_install_log.ckw.txt
    sudo rm -f $tmp_log
    set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
    MAX_RETRY=3
    TIMES=1
    while [ ${CMD_STATUS} -ne 0 ]; do
        cat $tmp_log > /dev/stderr
        if [ $TIMES -gt $MAX_RETRY ]; then
            echo "Unhandled error when running $1: `tr '\n' '; ' <$tmp_log`" 1>&2; exit 1
        fi
        if grep -q -e "is locked by another process" -e "E: Could not get lock /var/" -e "Could not open lock" $tmp_log; then
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting $2 seconds for apt to be available"; sleep ${2}
        elif grep -q -e "apt --fix-broken install" $tmp_log; then
            echo "safe apt install failed, need to run apt --fix-broken install"
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; sudo apt-get -f -y --allow-downgrades install 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting 5 seconds for apt"; sleep 5
        else
            echo "safe apt install failed: $1, exit code: $CMD_STATUS, retry number: $TIMES"
            echo "will sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ] seconds before next attempt"
            sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ]
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
        fi
    done
    if [ $CMD_STATUS -eq 0 ] && [ $TIMES -ne 1 ]; then
        echo "safe apt install succeed. retry number: $TIMES."
    fi
}
safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get -y update" 5
sleep 0.5
safe_apt_install "sudo -E apt-get -y --allow-downgrades  install /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/nv-tensorrt-local-repo-ubuntu2404-10.16.2-cuda-13.2_1.0-1_arm64.deb" 5
sleep 5
# ensure package version from local repository has a higher priority
sudo mkdir -p /etc/apt/preferences.d/
sudo rm -f /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm
sudo touch /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm
echo 'Package: *' | sudo tee -a /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm
echo 'Pin: origin ""' | sudo tee -a /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm
echo 'Pin-Priority: 999' | sudo tee -a /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm

packageName=$(dpkg -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/nv-tensorrt-local-repo-ubuntu2404-10.16.2-cuda-13.2_1.0-1_arm64.deb Package)
keyringFilePath=$(dpkg -L $packageName | grep 'keyring\.gpg$' | head -n 1 | tr -d "\n")
if [ -n "$keyringFilePath" ]
then
	if [ -f "$keyringFilePath" ]
	then
		sudo cp $keyringFilePath /usr/share/keyrings/
	else
		echo 'Warning: deb repo keyring file not found' >&2
	fi
else
	pubKeyFilePath=$(dpkg -L $packageName | grep '\.pub$' | head -n 1 | tr -d "\n")
	echo $pubKeyFilePath
	if [ -n "$pubKeyFilePath" ]
	then
		safe_apt_install "sudo apt-key add $pubKeyFilePath" 5
	else
		echo 'Warning: deb repo pub key file not found' >&2
	fi
fi
sleep 2
safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get -y update" 5

safe_apt_install "sudo -E apt-get -y  install tensorrt-libs " 5
targetDebRepoPackageName=$(dpkg -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/nv-tensorrt-local-repo-ubuntu2404-10.16.2-cuda-13.2_1.0-1_arm64.deb Package | tr -d '\n')
echo targetDebRepoPackageName $targetDebRepoPackageName
if [ -n "targetDebRepoPackageName" ]
then
    safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get purge -y $targetDebRepoPackageName" 5
else
    echo 'Warning: target deb repo package name not found' >&2
fi
sudo rm -f /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm

sudo rm -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/nv-tensorrt-local-repo-ubuntu2404-10.16.2-cuda-13.2_1.0-1_arm64.deb

#****** end of  /home/ckw/.nvsdkm/replays/scripts/JetPack_7.2_Linux/NV_L4T_TENSORRT_RUNTIME_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh ******


scp /home/ckw/.nvsdkm/replays/scripts/JetPack_7.2_Linux/NV_L4T_TENSORRT_RUNTIME_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh jetson@[192.168.55.1]:~; ssh -t jetson@192.168.55.1 "set -o pipefail; ~/NV_L4T_TENSORRT_RUNTIME_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh | sed 's/^/SDKM_INSTALL_ERROR /' && rm -f ~/NV_L4T_TENSORRT_RUNTIME_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh"
cd /tmp
ssh jetson@192.168.55.1 "dpkg-query -W -f='\${db:Status-abbrev} \${Version}' tensorrt-libs"
ssh   jetson@192.168.55.1 "df -B1 / | tail -1 | awk '{ printf \$4 }'"
export _xdk_type=

#INFO: Start to install NV_L4T_CUDNN_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP
ssh   jetson@192.168.55.1 "df -B1 / | tail -1 | awk '{ printf \$4 }'"

            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
        fi
    done
    if [ $CMD_STATUS -eq 0 ] && [ $TIMES -ne 1 ]; then

#!/bin/bash
set -e
export LC_ALL=en_US.UTF-8 LANG=en_US.UTF-8 LANGUAGE=en_US.UTF-8

exec 3>&1
exec 1>&2
exec 2>&3
function safe_apt_install {
    export DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive
    echo "*begin $1, $2"
    tmp_log=/tmp/sdkm_apt_install_log.ckw.txt
    sudo rm -f $tmp_log
    set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
    MAX_RETRY=3
    TIMES=1
    while [ ${CMD_STATUS} -ne 0 ]; do
        cat $tmp_log > /dev/stderr
        if [ $TIMES -gt $MAX_RETRY ]; then
            echo "Unhandled error when running $1: `tr '\n' '; ' <$tmp_log`" 1>&2; exit 1
        fi
        if grep -q -e "is locked by another process" -e "E: Could not get lock /var/" -e "Could not open lock" $tmp_log; then
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting $2 seconds for apt to be available"; sleep ${2}
        elif grep -q -e "apt --fix-broken install" $tmp_log; then
            echo "safe apt install failed, need to run apt --fix-broken install"
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; sudo apt-get -f -y --allow-downgrades install 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting 5 seconds for apt"; sleep 5
        else
            echo "safe apt install failed: $1, exit code: $CMD_STATUS, retry number: $TIMES"
            echo "will sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ] seconds before next attempt"
            sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ]
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
        fi
    done
    if [ $CMD_STATUS -eq 0 ] && [ $TIMES -ne 1 ]; then
        echo "safe apt install succeed. retry number: $TIMES."
    fi
}
safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get -y update" 5
sleep 0.5
safe_apt_install "sudo -E apt-get -y --allow-downgrades  install /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/holoscan-cuda-13_4.4.0.1-1_arm64.deb" 5
sleep 5
sudo rm -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/holoscan-cuda-13_4.4.0.1-1_arm64.deb

#!/bin/bash
set -e
export LC_ALL=en_US.UTF-8 LANG=en_US.UTF-8 LANGUAGE=en_US.UTF-8

exec 3>&1
exec 1>&2
exec 2>&3
function safe_apt_install {
    export DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive
    echo "*begin $1, $2"
    tmp_log=/tmp/sdkm_apt_install_log.ckw.txt
    sudo rm -f $tmp_log
    set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
    MAX_RETRY=3
    TIMES=1
    while [ ${CMD_STATUS} -ne 0 ]; do
        cat $tmp_log > /dev/stderr
        if [ $TIMES -gt $MAX_RETRY ]; then
            echo "Unhandled error when running $1: `tr '\n' '; ' <$tmp_log`" 1>&2; exit 1
        fi
        if grep -q -e "is locked by another process" -e "E: Could not get lock /var/" -e "Could not open lock" $tmp_log; then
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting $2 seconds for apt to be available"; sleep ${2}
        elif grep -q -e "apt --fix-broken install" $tmp_log; then
            echo "safe apt install failed, need to run apt --fix-broken install"
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; sudo apt-get -f -y --allow-downgrades install 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting 5 seconds for apt"; sleep 5
        else
            echo "safe apt install failed: $1, exit code: $CMD_STATUS, retry number: $TIMES"
            echo "will sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ] seconds before next attempt"
            sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ]
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
        fi
    done
    if [ $CMD_STATUS -eq 0 ] && [ $TIMES -ne 1 ]; then
        echo "safe apt install succeed. retry number: $TIMES."
    fi
}
safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get -y update" 5
sleep 0.5
safe_apt_install "sudo -E apt-get -y --allow-downgrades  install /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/libnvidia-container1_1.19.1-1_arm64.deb" 5
sleep 5
sudo rm -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/libnvidia-container1_1.19.1-1_arm64.deb

#****** end of  /home/ckw/.nvsdkm/replays/scripts/JetPack_7.2_Linux/NV_L4T_DOCKER_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh ******

cd /tmp
ssh jetson@192.168.55.1 "dpkg-query -W -f='\${db:Status-abbrev} \${Version}' libnvidia-container1"

            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; sudo apt-get -f -y --allow-downgrades install 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting 5 seconds for apt"; sleep 5
        else
            echo "safe apt install failed: $1, exit code: $CMD_STATUS, retry number: $TIMES"
            echo "will sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ] seconds before next attempt"
            sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ]
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
        fi
    done
    if [ $CMD_STATUS -eq 0 ] && [ $TIMES -ne 1 ]; then
        echo "safe apt install succeed. retry number: $TIMES."
    fi
}
safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get -y update" 5
sleep 0.5
safe_apt_install "sudo -E apt-get -y --allow-downgrades  install /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/cuda-repo-ubuntu2404-13-2-local_13.2.1-595.58.03-1_arm64.deb" 5
sleep 5
# ensure package version from local repository has a higher priority
sudo mkdir -p /etc/apt/preferences.d/
sudo rm -f /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm
sudo touch /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm
echo 'Package: *' | sudo tee -a /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm
echo 'Pin: origin ""' | sudo tee -a /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm
echo 'Pin-Priority: 999' | sudo tee -a /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm

packageName=$(dpkg -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/cuda-repo-ubuntu2404-13-2-local_13.2.1-595.58.03-1_arm64.deb Package)
keyringFilePath=$(dpkg -L $packageName | grep 'keyring\.gpg$' | head -n 1 | tr -d "\n")
if [ -n "$keyringFilePath" ]
then
	if [ -f "$keyringFilePath" ]
	then
		sudo cp $keyringFilePath /usr/share/keyrings/
	else
		echo 'Warning: deb repo keyring file not found' >&2
	fi
else
	pubKeyFilePath=$(dpkg -L $packageName | grep '\.pub$' | head -n 1 | tr -d "\n")
	echo $pubKeyFilePath
	if [ -n "$pubKeyFilePath" ]
	then
		safe_apt_install "sudo apt-key add $pubKeyFilePath" 5
	else
		echo 'Warning: deb repo pub key file not found' >&2
	fi
fi
sleep 2
safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get -y update" 5

safe_apt_install "sudo -E apt-get -y  install cuda-cudart-dev-13-2 cuda-driver-dev-13-2 " 5
targetDebRepoPackageName=$(dpkg -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/cuda-repo-ubuntu2404-13-2-local_13.2.1-595.58.03-1_arm64.deb Package | tr -d '\n')
echo targetDebRepoPackageName $targetDebRepoPackageName
if [ -n "targetDebRepoPackageName" ]
then
    safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get purge -y $targetDebRepoPackageName" 5
else
    echo 'Warning: target deb repo package name not found' >&2
fi
sudo rm -f /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm

sudo rm -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/cuda-repo-ubuntu2404-13-2-local_13.2.1-595.58.03-1_arm64.deb

#****** end of  /home/ckw/.nvsdkm/replays/scripts/JetPack_7.2_Linux/NV_L4T_CUDA_RUNTIME_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh ******

cd /tmp

#!/bin/bash
set -e
export LC_ALL=en_US.UTF-8 LANG=en_US.UTF-8 LANGUAGE=en_US.UTF-8

exec 3>&1
exec 1>&2
exec 2>&3
function safe_apt_install {
    export DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive
    echo "*begin $1, $2"
    tmp_log=/tmp/sdkm_apt_install_log.ckw.txt
    sudo rm -f $tmp_log
    set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
    MAX_RETRY=3
    TIMES=1
    while [ ${CMD_STATUS} -ne 0 ]; do
        cat $tmp_log > /dev/stderr
        if [ $TIMES -gt $MAX_RETRY ]; then
            echo "Unhandled error when running $1: `tr '\n' '; ' <$tmp_log`" 1>&2; exit 1
        fi
        if grep -q -e "is locked by another process" -e "E: Could not get lock /var/" -e "Could not open lock" $tmp_log; then
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting $2 seconds for apt to be available"; sleep ${2}
        elif grep -q -e "apt --fix-broken install" $tmp_log; then
            echo "safe apt install failed, need to run apt --fix-broken install"
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; sudo apt-get -f -y --allow-downgrades install 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting 5 seconds for apt"; sleep 5
        else
            echo "safe apt install failed: $1, exit code: $CMD_STATUS, retry number: $TIMES"
            echo "will sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ] seconds before next attempt"
            sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ]
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
        fi
    done
    if [ $CMD_STATUS -eq 0 ] && [ $TIMES -ne 1 ]; then
        echo "safe apt install succeed. retry number: $TIMES."
    fi
}
safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get -y update" 5
sleep 0.5
safe_apt_install "sudo -E apt-get -y --allow-downgrades  install /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/nvidia-l4t-cudadebuggingsupport_13.2-37290934.0_arm64.deb" 5
sleep 5
sudo rm -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/nvidia-l4t-cudadebuggingsupport_13.2-37290934.0_arm64.deb

#****** end of  /home/ckw/.nvsdkm/replays/scripts/JetPack_7.2_Linux/NV_L4T_CUDA_RUNTIME_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh ******

#!/bin/bash
set -e
export LC_ALL=en_US.UTF-8 LANG=en_US.UTF-8 LANGUAGE=en_US.UTF-8

exec 3>&1
exec 1>&2
exec 2>&3
function safe_apt_install {
    export DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive
    echo "*begin $1, $2"
    tmp_log=/tmp/sdkm_apt_install_log.ckw.txt
    sudo rm -f $tmp_log
    set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
    MAX_RETRY=3
    TIMES=1
    while [ ${CMD_STATUS} -ne 0 ]; do
        cat $tmp_log > /dev/stderr
        if [ $TIMES -gt $MAX_RETRY ]; then
            echo "Unhandled error when running $1: `tr '\n' '; ' <$tmp_log`" 1>&2; exit 1
        fi
        if grep -q -e "is locked by another process" -e "E: Could not get lock /var/" -e "Could not open lock" $tmp_log; then
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting $2 seconds for apt to be available"; sleep ${2}
        elif grep -q -e "apt --fix-broken install" $tmp_log; then
            echo "safe apt install failed, need to run apt --fix-broken install"
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; sudo apt-get -f -y --allow-downgrades install 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting 5 seconds for apt"; sleep 5
        else
            echo "safe apt install failed: $1, exit code: $CMD_STATUS, retry number: $TIMES"
            echo "will sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ] seconds before next attempt"
            sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ]
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
        fi
    done
    if [ $CMD_STATUS -eq 0 ] && [ $TIMES -ne 1 ]; then
        echo "safe apt install succeed. retry number: $TIMES."
    fi
}
safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get -y update" 5
sleep 0.5
safe_apt_install "sudo -E apt-get -y --allow-downgrades  install /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/vpi-lib-4.1.3-aarch64-l4t.deb" 5
sleep 5
sudo rm -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/vpi-lib-4.1.3-aarch64-l4t.deb

#****** end of  /home/ckw/.nvsdkm/replays/scripts/JetPack_7.2_Linux/NV_L4T_VPI_RUNTIME_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh ******

cd /tmp

            echo "Unhandled error when running $1: `tr '\n' '; ' <$tmp_log`" 1>&2; exit 1
        fi
        if grep -q -e "is locked by another process" -e "E: Could not get lock /var/" -e "Could not open lock" $tmp_log; then
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting $2 seconds for apt to be available"; sleep ${2}
        elif grep -q -e "apt --fix-broken install" $tmp_log; then
            echo "safe apt install failed, need to run apt --fix-broken install"
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; sudo apt-get -f -y --allow-downgrades install 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting 5 seconds for apt"; sleep 5
        else
            echo "safe apt install failed: $1, exit code: $CMD_STATUS, retry number: $TIMES"
            echo "will sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ] seconds before next attempt"
            sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ]
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
        fi
    done
    if [ $CMD_STATUS -eq 0 ] && [ $TIMES -ne 1 ]; then
        echo "safe apt install succeed. retry number: $TIMES."
    fi
}
safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get -y update" 5
sleep 0.5

sleep 5

#!/bin/bash
set -e
export LC_ALL=en_US.UTF-8 LANG=en_US.UTF-8 LANGUAGE=en_US.UTF-8

exec 3>&1
exec 1>&2
exec 2>&3
function safe_apt_install {
    export DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive
    echo "*begin $1, $2"
    tmp_log=/tmp/sdkm_apt_install_log.ckw.txt
    sudo rm -f $tmp_log
    set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
    MAX_RETRY=3
    TIMES=1
    while [ ${CMD_STATUS} -ne 0 ]; do
        cat $tmp_log > /dev/stderr
        if [ $TIMES -gt $MAX_RETRY ]; then
            echo "Unhandled error when running $1: `tr '\n' '; ' <$tmp_log`" 1>&2; exit 1
        fi
        if grep -q -e "is locked by another process" -e "E: Could not get lock /var/" -e "Could not open lock" $tmp_log; then
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting $2 seconds for apt to be available"; sleep ${2}
        elif grep -q -e "apt --fix-broken install" $tmp_log; then
            echo "safe apt install failed, need to run apt --fix-broken install"
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; sudo apt-get -f -y --allow-downgrades install 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting 5 seconds for apt"; sleep 5
        else
            echo "safe apt install failed: $1, exit code: $CMD_STATUS, retry number: $TIMES"
            echo "will sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ] seconds before next attempt"
            sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ]
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
        fi
    done
    if [ $CMD_STATUS -eq 0 ] && [ $TIMES -ne 1 ]; then
        echo "safe apt install succeed. retry number: $TIMES."
    fi
}
safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get -y update" 5
sleep 0.5
safe_apt_install "sudo -E apt-get -y --allow-downgrades  install /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/cuda-repo-ubuntu2404-13-2-local_13.2.1-595.58.03-1_arm64.deb" 5
sleep 5
# ensure package version from local repository has a higher priority
sudo mkdir -p /etc/apt/preferences.d/
sudo rm -f /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm
sudo touch /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm
echo 'Package: *' | sudo tee -a /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm
echo 'Pin: origin ""' | sudo tee -a /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm
echo 'Pin-Priority: 999' | sudo tee -a /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm

packageName=$(dpkg -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/cuda-repo-ubuntu2404-13-2-local_13.2.1-595.58.03-1_arm64.deb Package)
keyringFilePath=$(dpkg -L $packageName | grep 'keyring\.gpg$' | head -n 1 | tr -d "\n")
if [ -n "$keyringFilePath" ]
then
	if [ -f "$keyringFilePath" ]
	then
		sudo cp $keyringFilePath /usr/share/keyrings/
	else
		echo 'Warning: deb repo keyring file not found' >&2
	fi
else
	pubKeyFilePath=$(dpkg -L $packageName | grep '\.pub$' | head -n 1 | tr -d "\n")
	echo $pubKeyFilePath
	if [ -n "$pubKeyFilePath" ]
	then
		safe_apt_install "sudo apt-key add $pubKeyFilePath" 5
	else
		echo 'Warning: deb repo pub key file not found' >&2
	fi
fi
sleep 2
safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get -y update" 5

safe_apt_install "sudo -E apt-get -y  install cuda-toolkit-13-2 " 5
targetDebRepoPackageName=$(dpkg -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/cuda-repo-ubuntu2404-13-2-local_13.2.1-595.58.03-1_arm64.deb Package | tr -d '\n')
echo targetDebRepoPackageName $targetDebRepoPackageName
if [ -n "targetDebRepoPackageName" ]
then
    safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get purge -y $targetDebRepoPackageName" 5
else
    echo 'Warning: target deb repo package name not found' >&2
fi
sudo rm -f /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm

sudo rm -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/cuda-repo-ubuntu2404-13-2-local_13.2.1-595.58.03-1_arm64.deb

#!/bin/bash
set -e
export LC_ALL=en_US.UTF-8 LANG=en_US.UTF-8 LANGUAGE=en_US.UTF-8

exec 3>&1
exec 1>&2
exec 2>&3
function safe_apt_install {
    export DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive
    echo "*begin $1, $2"
    tmp_log=/tmp/sdkm_apt_install_log.ckw.txt
    sudo rm -f $tmp_log
    set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
    MAX_RETRY=3
    TIMES=1
    while [ ${CMD_STATUS} -ne 0 ]; do
        cat $tmp_log > /dev/stderr
        if [ $TIMES -gt $MAX_RETRY ]; then
            echo "Unhandled error when running $1: `tr '\n' '; ' <$tmp_log`" 1>&2; exit 1
        fi
        if grep -q -e "is locked by another process" -e "E: Could not get lock /var/" -e "Could not open lock" $tmp_log; then
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting $2 seconds for apt to be available"; sleep ${2}
        elif grep -q -e "apt --fix-broken install" $tmp_log; then
            echo "safe apt install failed, need to run apt --fix-broken install"
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; sudo apt-get -f -y --allow-downgrades install 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting 5 seconds for apt"; sleep 5
        else
            echo "safe apt install failed: $1, exit code: $CMD_STATUS, retry number: $TIMES"
            echo "will sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ] seconds before next attempt"
            sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ]
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
        fi
    done
    if [ $CMD_STATUS -eq 0 ] && [ $TIMES -ne 1 ]; then
        echo "safe apt install succeed. retry number: $TIMES."
    fi
}
safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get -y update" 5
sleep 0.5
safe_apt_install "sudo -E apt-get -y --allow-downgrades  install /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/nv-tensorrt-local-repo-ubuntu2404-10.16.2-cuda-13.2_1.0-1_arm64.deb" 5
sleep 5
# ensure package version from local repository has a higher priority
sudo mkdir -p /etc/apt/preferences.d/
sudo rm -f /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm
sudo touch /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm
echo 'Package: *' | sudo tee -a /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm
echo 'Pin: origin ""' | sudo tee -a /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm
echo 'Pin-Priority: 999' | sudo tee -a /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm

packageName=$(dpkg -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/nv-tensorrt-local-repo-ubuntu2404-10.16.2-cuda-13.2_1.0-1_arm64.deb Package)
keyringFilePath=$(dpkg -L $packageName | grep 'keyring\.gpg$' | head -n 1 | tr -d "\n")
if [ -n "$keyringFilePath" ]
then
	if [ -f "$keyringFilePath" ]
	then
		sudo cp $keyringFilePath /usr/share/keyrings/
	else
		echo 'Warning: deb repo keyring file not found' >&2
	fi
else
	pubKeyFilePath=$(dpkg -L $packageName | grep '\.pub$' | head -n 1 | tr -d "\n")
	echo $pubKeyFilePath
	if [ -n "$pubKeyFilePath" ]
	then
		safe_apt_install "sudo apt-key add $pubKeyFilePath" 5
	else
		echo 'Warning: deb repo pub key file not found' >&2
	fi
fi
sleep 2
safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get -y update" 5

safe_apt_install "sudo -E apt-get -y  install tensorrt-libs " 5
targetDebRepoPackageName=$(dpkg -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/nv-tensorrt-local-repo-ubuntu2404-10.16.2-cuda-13.2_1.0-1_arm64.deb Package | tr -d '\n')
echo targetDebRepoPackageName $targetDebRepoPackageName
if [ -n "targetDebRepoPackageName" ]
then
    safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get purge -y $targetDebRepoPackageName" 5
else
    echo 'Warning: target deb repo package name not found' >&2
fi
sudo rm -f /etc/apt/preferences.d/sdkm

sudo rm -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/nv-tensorrt-local-repo-ubuntu2404-10.16.2-cuda-13.2_1.0-1_arm64.deb

#****** end of  /home/ckw/.nvsdkm/replays/scripts/JetPack_7.2_Linux/NV_L4T_TENSORRT_RUNTIME_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh ******


scp /home/ckw/.nvsdkm/replays/scripts/JetPack_7.2_Linux/NV_L4T_TENSORRT_RUNTIME_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh jetson@[192.168.55.1]:~; ssh -t jetson@192.168.55.1 "set -o pipefail; ~/NV_L4T_TENSORRT_RUNTIME_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh | sed 's/^/SDKM_INSTALL_ERROR /' && rm -f ~/NV_L4T_TENSORRT_RUNTIME_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP.sh"
cd /tmp
ssh jetson@192.168.55.1 "dpkg-query -W -f='\${db:Status-abbrev} \${Version}' tensorrt-libs"
ssh   jetson@192.168.55.1 "df -B1 / | tail -1 | awk '{ printf \$4 }'"
export _xdk_type=

#INFO: Start to install NV_L4T_CUDNN_SBSA_TARGET_POST_INSTALL_COMP
ssh   jetson@192.168.55.1 "df -B1 / | tail -1 | awk '{ printf \$4 }'"

            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; sudo apt-get -f -y --allow-downgrades install 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting 5 seconds for apt"; sleep 5
        else
            echo "safe apt install failed: $1, exit code: $CMD_STATUS, retry number: $TIMES"
            echo "will sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ] seconds before next attempt"
            sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ]
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
        fi
    done
    if [ $CMD_STATUS -eq 0 ] && [ $TIMES -ne 1 ]; then
        echo "safe apt install succeed. retry number: $TIMES."
    fi
}
safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get -y update" 5
sleep 0.5

#!/bin/bash
set -e
export LC_ALL=en_US.UTF-8 LANG=en_US.UTF-8 LANGUAGE=en_US.UTF-8

exec 3>&1
exec 1>&2
exec 2>&3
function safe_apt_install {
    export DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive
    echo "*begin $1, $2"
    tmp_log=/tmp/sdkm_apt_install_log.ckw.txt
    sudo rm -f $tmp_log
    set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
    MAX_RETRY=3
    TIMES=1
    while [ ${CMD_STATUS} -ne 0 ]; do
        cat $tmp_log > /dev/stderr
        if [ $TIMES -gt $MAX_RETRY ]; then
            echo "Unhandled error when running $1: `tr '\n' '; ' <$tmp_log`" 1>&2; exit 1
        fi
        if grep -q -e "is locked by another process" -e "E: Could not get lock /var/" -e "Could not open lock" $tmp_log; then
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting $2 seconds for apt to be available"; sleep ${2}
        elif grep -q -e "apt --fix-broken install" $tmp_log; then
            echo "safe apt install failed, need to run apt --fix-broken install"
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; sudo apt-get -f -y --allow-downgrades install 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
            echo "Waiting 5 seconds for apt"; sleep 5
        else
            echo "safe apt install failed: $1, exit code: $CMD_STATUS, retry number: $TIMES"
            echo "will sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ] seconds before next attempt"
            sleep $[ $TIMES*10 ]
            TIMES=$[ $TIMES + 1 ]
            sudo rm -f $tmp_log
            set +e; $1 2>$tmp_log; CMD_STATUS=${PIPESTATUS[0]}; set -e
        fi
    done
    if [ $CMD_STATUS -eq 0 ] && [ $TIMES -ne 1 ]; then
        echo "safe apt install succeed. retry number: $TIMES."
    fi
}
safe_apt_install "sudo apt-get -y update" 5
sleep 0.5
safe_apt_install "sudo -E apt-get -y --allow-downgrades  install /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/holoscan-cuda-13_4.4.0.1-1_arm64.deb" 5
sleep 5
sudo rm -f /opt/nvidia/deb_repos/holoscan-cuda-13_4.4.0.1-1_arm64.deb
